# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
suppose a 25 year old person is offered an account paying 5% compounded continuesly ^WhKsd78U

what about decay?
half life of an element is the amount of time it takes for the quantity to be halved
example problem might use radiocarbon dating
carbon 14 decays at an exponential rate with a half life of 5730 years
say I was walking around and i found an artifact originally with 100g of carbon 14, and i measure today and find it has 10g of carbon 14 how old is it? ^0L3cs3Nz
